type="application/ld+json">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@graph":[{"@type":"FAQPage","mainEntity":[{"@type":"Question","name":"What are the pros and cons of earned media?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Earned media delivers increased brand awareness, brand credibility, and performance without adding to your advertising budget. It builds trust through third-party content like reviews, press coverage, or influencer endorsements. The downside? It’s unpredictable. You can’t fully control message timing, tone, or reach, and results often rely on consistent content creation and strong communication channels. "}},{"@type":"Question","name":"What is the difference between EMV and CPM?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"EMV reflects the value generated through attention gained without direct cost—often across third-party sites or influencer campaigns.  It measures the dollar amount equivalent of exposure from unpaid sources like news articles, social mentions, or word-of-mouth recommendations. Although often used to calculate earned media value, CPM is more commonly used as a bidding strategy which calculates advertising costs per thousand impressions. "}},{"@type":"Question","name":"What are the five most common examples of earned media?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Typical examples include: News articles and press coverage from media outlets Mentions or reposts on social channels Online reviews and third-party posts Influencer endorsements or creator economy features Organic search activity "}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Why is earned media a valuable metric to track?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Earned media content is often used to drive brand credibility and increase brand awareness at minimal cost. It delivers authentic voice through third‑party mentions and online reviews that often is typically more authentic than paid campaigns. Tracking EMV provides meaningful performance metrics by quantifying the reach and impact of non-paid media impressions. "}},{"@type":"Question","name":"How do earned media campaigns strengthen a brand’s credibility?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Campaigns that result in third-party content or influencer endorsements give weight to your client’s brand, boosting perception of authenticity and trust. Positive press coverage, online reviews, or social mentions create meaningful connections with potential customers—beyond what advertising alone can achieve. "}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Can earned media value calculations align with social media strategy and influencer campaigns?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Absolutely. Earned media campaign ROI can be calculated by applying EMV formulas—like EMV impressions × CPM rate—to social media content or influencer endorsements. This approach quantifies the value of organic engagement in a way that complements paid social ads and integrates smoothly with your broader social media marketing strategy. "}},{"@type":"Question","name":"How do adjustment factors impact earned media value?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Adjustment factors help fine-tune EMV to reflect campaign objectives, content quality, and context—whether that content appears on news outlets, third-party sites, or social media channels. These modifiers ensure that the calculated dollar amount reflects differences in media impressions, the brand’s image, or the quality of earned media content. "}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Why do some earned media campaigns deliver better engagement than paid efforts?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Because earned media’s power lies in audience trust—content earned through relationships or positive sentiment often receives more attention than paid placements. Earned media creates authentic exposure across third-party mentions and social channels, enhancing customer connections with a real voice and delivering stronger engagement metrics than many paid tactics. "}}]},{"@type":"Review","author":{"@type":"Person","name":"Mark Mehler","worksFor":{"@type":"Organization","name":"Back2Black Marketing Agency"}},"datePublished":"2023-01-18T16:50:19.377Z","reviewBody":"This is exactly the type of tool I have been looking for. class="Section-module__O9PlXa__subheading"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth" style="--maxWidth:none"><div class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-normal leading-normal text-lg text-left" style="margin-bottom:0"><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__text"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Earned Media Value provides a monetary assessment of a brand&#x27;s non-paid exposure. This metric is essential for understanding organic reach and brand sentiment. It&#x27;s a crucial indicator of whether the public views a brand favorably and helps predict future growth.</p><p>EMV also serves a strategic purpose. It informs resource allocation by identifying which channels and campaigns are most effective in generating organic engagement. High EMV often correlates with brand awareness and customer loyalty, leading to increased revenue over time.</p><p>Tracking this metric provides actionable insights for optimizing marketing campaigns.</p></div></div></div></div></div></header></div></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__mediaSpacer"></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__media" class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth" style="--maxWidth:none"><h2 class="text-zinc-900 font-geologica font-medium leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl text-left" style="margin-bottom:0"><span>How Earned Media Value Complements Other KPIs</span></h2></div></div><div class="Section-module__O9PlXa__subheading"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth" style="--maxWidth:none"><div class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-normal leading-normal text-lg text-left" style="margin-bottom:0"><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__text"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Earned Media Value works with other critical metrics like impressions, reach, keyword rankings, engagement, and click-through rates (CTR). A high EMV usually suggests that content is resonating, which often translates to better engagement and more clicks. It&#x27;s a direct line to understanding campaign success.</p><p><br/></p><p>EMV also ties into Return on Investment (ROI), especially when evaluating the efficiency of paid campaigns. A robust EMV often leads to a more favorable ROI by increasing organic reach and reducing acquisition costs. Monitoring EMV alongside these other metrics generates a clearer, more actionable view of campaign performance.</p><p><br/><br/><br/></p></div></div></div></div></div></header></div></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__mediaSpacer"></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__media DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__reversed" justify-start w-full"><div style="letter-spacing:0.05em">Understanding the Progression</div></div></div></div></div><div><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth" style="--maxWidth:none"><h2 class="text-zinc-900 font-geologica font-medium leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl text-left" style="margin-bottom:0"><span>The Evolution of Earned Media Value in the Digital Era</span></h2></div></div><div class="Section-module__O9PlXa__subheading"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth" style="--maxWidth:none"><div class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-normal leading-normal text-lg text-left" style="margin-bottom:0"><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__text"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Initially, EMV was focused on traditional media mentions, but the advent of social media platforms shifted the paradigm. This digital transition marked a significant change in marketing efforts, emphasizing the value of organic shares, mentions, and engagement instead of purely looking at paid advertising on social platforms. Today, a successful social media campaign hinges not just on content, but on how effectively it can generate earned media.</p><p>The incorporation of an influencer marketing strategy into the marketing toolbox further revolutionized EMV. Leveraging personalities with substantial followings on platforms like Instagram and YouTube, has become a cornerstone in efforts to generate earned media. The synergy between influencer marketing and the other elements of an earned media strategy underscores the shift towards valuing authentic, user-generated content over traditional advertising methods.</p></div></div></div></div></div></header></div></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__mediaSpacer"></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__media" style="background:var(--color-undefined);border-radius:12px;padding:0"><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__imageWrap" style="width:auto"><img alt="Other Impactor KPI Factors to Consider" w-full"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="quote-left" class="svg-inline--fa fa-quote-left fa-xl " role="img" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 448 512" color="var(--color-zinc-900)"><path fill="currentColor" d="M0 216C0 149.7 53.7 96 120 96l8 0c17.7 0 32 14.3 32 32s-14.3 32-32 32l-8 0c-30.9 0-56 25.1-56 56l0 8 64 0c35.3 0 64 28.7 64 64l0 64c0 35.3-28.7 64-64 64l-64 0c-35.3 0-64-28.7-64-64l0-32 0-32 0-72z"></path></svg><blockquote class="Quote-module__qo9HCa__quoteText">KPIs are just an extension of us living out our core values. KPIs keep us accountable. KPIs provide the client transparency into what work we deliver and, most importantly... KPIs demonstrate that we are on track and achieving mutually agreed upon goals - this is our agency’s true north “results for clients.”</blockquote><div><div class="font-dm-sans text-lg leading-normal font-bold text-zinc-900">David Krauter</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div id="formula-section"><section data-section-bg="zinc-900" class="Section-module__O9PlXa__section Section-module__O9PlXa__padding-top-default-default Section-module__O9PlXa__padding-bottom-default-default bg-zinc-900 Section-module__O9PlXa__border-radius-none" style="color:var(--color-white);position:relative;overflow:visible"><div class="Container-module__V75Bgq__container"><div class="flex gap-40 flex-col w-full"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:56rem"><header class="flex gap-12 flex-col w-full"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:30rem"><div class=""><div class="text-brand-500 font-dm-sans font-semibold leading-tight text-sm md:text-md text-center uppercase">Formula</div></div></div><div><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:65rem"><h2 class="text-white font-geologica font-medium leading-tight text-3xl md:text-4xl text-center" style="margin-bottom:0">How To Calculate Earned Media Value</h2></div></div><div class="grid gap-xs text-center"><p>The formula for calculating Earned Media Value varies depending on the specific metrics a brand chooses to focus on. Still, it often revolves around assigning a dollar value to overall impressions or different types of engagements. </p><p>This could mean assigning values to likes, shares, comments, and even the reach or impressions a piece of content garners. Each type of engagement has its own weight. Likes indicate general approval, while shares and comments often suggest a deeper level of engagement. Impressions give the agency a sense of reach but don&#x27;t necessarily indicate interaction. Choose the types of engagements that align with the brand&#x27;s goals.</p><p>These dollar values are set based on the estimated impact every kind of engagement has on brand awareness, customer acquisition, or other goals.<br/><br/>After assigning values to each engagement type, multiply these by the number of engagements received for each type. Determine the sum to get the total EMV for a specific post or campaign. Keep in mind these values aren&#x27;t set in stone. Adjust them based on the current impact and objectives of the campaign for the most accurate results.</p></div></header></div><div class="FormulaSection-module__jiRzCG__equationContainer"><div class="flex gap-40 flex-col w-full"><p class="text-center font-dm-sans text-3xl leading-tight font-bold text-inherit" style="margin-bottom:0">Earned Media Formula Example</p><div FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__equal"><span>=</span></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__equal"><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__group class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><div><span>Likes</span></div></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__operator FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><span>X</span></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><div><span>Value per Like</span></div></div></div></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__operator FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__add"><span>+</span></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__add"><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__operator FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><span>X</span></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><div><span>Value per Share</span></div></div></div></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__operator FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__add"><span>+</span></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__add"><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__group FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><div><span>Comments</span></div></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__operator FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><span>X</span></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><div><span>Value per Comment</span></div></div></div></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__operator FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__add"><span>+</span></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__add"><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__group FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><div><span>Impressions</span></div></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__operator FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><span>X</span></div><div class="FormulaEquation-module__OVV1GW__multiply"><div><span>Value per Impression</span></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="Grid-module__x1ukXq__grid Grid-module__x1ukXq__gap-default-md" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(12, 1fr)"><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-lg-6" style="position:relative"><h3 class="font-geologica text-3xl font-medium text-inherit">What Is a Good Average Earned Media Value?</h3><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>An excellent average EMV is often several times higher than the amount spent on paid media in the same campaign. For instance, if $1,000 is spent on paid social posts, an EMV of $4,000 or higher is generally considered successful, indicating the campaign has a high organic impact.<br/><br/></p></div></div><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-lg-6" style="position:relative"><h3 class="font-geologica text-3xl font-medium text-inherit">What Is a Bad Average Earned Media Value?</h3><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>A bad EMV would be low, especially one near or below the paid media spend. If $1,000 is invested in paid promotions and the EMV is only $1,200, it&#x27;s time to rethink the strategy. This suggests low organic reach and little consumer interest in the content.</p><p><br/><br/><br/></p></div></div><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12" style="position:relative"><div Clients</span></h2></div></div><div class="Section-module__O9PlXa__subheading"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth" style="--maxWidth:none"><div class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-normal leading-normal text-lg text-left" style="margin-bottom:0"><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__text"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>For clients, Earned Media Value is a key metric that closely aligns with their broader business objectives.</p><p><br/></p><p>It serves as a tangible indicator of brand buzz and a sign that the brand is resonating in the market, potentially leading to higher customer retention rates and even increased sales. </p><p><br/></p><p>A high EMV signifies that the public sees the brand and actively engages with it. It acts like a pulse check on a campaign&#x27;s resonance with consumers, offering a quick, digestible metric that translates to brand health.</p><p><br/></p><p>This kind of clarity is not just comforting; it&#x27;s actionable. Clients use these insights to reallocate resources or adjust their strategy to keep that EMV climbing, ensuring that their marketing objectives are not just met, but exceeded.</p><p><br/><br/><br/></p></div></div></div></div></div></header></div></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__mediaSpacer"></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__media class="text-zinc-900 font-geologica font-medium leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl text-left" style="margin-bottom:0"><span>Why Earned Media Value Matters to Agencies</span></h2></div></div><div class="Section-module__O9PlXa__subheading"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth" style="--maxWidth:none"><div class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-normal leading-normal text-lg text-left" style="margin-bottom:0"><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__text"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>For agencies, Earned Media Value is more than just a badge of creative honor; it is a critical performance indicator that makes or breaks client relationships. </p><p>A rising EMV demonstrates the efficacy of an agency&#x27;s creative and strategic efforts and speaks volumes about its ability to drive real business results. It essentially offers an objective measure of how well the agency&#x27;s storytelling and targeting resonate with the intended audience. </p><p>Additionally<!-- -->, leveraging a strong EMV secures future projects and enhances the agency&#x27;s reputation for delivering high ROI. </p><p>It&#x27;s not just about validating the creative team’s brilliance but providing concrete evidence that the agency&#x27;s approach is hitting the mark. By keeping an eye on EMV, agencies get a snapshot of their work&#x27;s quality and impact, enabling them to make data-driven decisions that keep clients happy and engaged.</p></div></div></div></div></div></header></div></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__mediaSpacer"></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__media" class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth" style="--maxWidth:none"><h2 class="text-zinc-900 font-geologica font-medium leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl text-left" style="margin-bottom:0"><span>The Complexities of Measuring EMV</span></h2></div></div><div class="Section-module__O9PlXa__subheading"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth" style="--maxWidth:none"><div class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-normal leading-normal text-lg text-left" style="margin-bottom:0"><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__text"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Measuring Earned Media Value presents unique challenges due to its inherent subjectivity. Assigning a monetary value to social media posts and user interactions is not straightforward. Unlike paid media efforts or owned media, where costs and returns are clearer, EMV relies on estimating the value of organic engagement, which can vary significantly between brands and campaigns.</p><p>Another challenge when trying to accurately measure earned media is isolating the impact of earned media performance from other marketing efforts. It&#x27;s difficult to determine whether a spike in engagement or brand popularity is solely due to effective organic strategies or if it&#x27;s influenced by concurrent paid or owned media activities. This overlap can muddy the waters, complicating efforts to quantify the true impact of EMV in terms of marketing dollars.</p><p>While EMV is a valuable metric, it requires careful consideration and a nuanced approach to measurement. </p></div></div></div></div></div></header></div></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__mediaSpacer"></div><div class="DetailItem-module__BA6uuG__media class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:56rem"><header class="flex gap-12 flex-col w-full"><div><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:65rem"><h2 class="text-zinc-900 font-geologica font-medium leading-tight text-3xl md:text-4xl text-center" style="margin-bottom:0">Making Sense of Earned Media Value for Smarter Campaigns</h2></div></div><p class="text-center font-dm-sans text-lg leading-normal font-normal text-inherit">Understanding Earned Media Value through multiple lenses is crucial. A thorough analysis not only validates past actions but also informs future strategies. In short, dissecting EMV yields better bang for the advertising buck.</p></header></div><div class="Section-module__O9PlXa__body" style="text-align:center"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:65rem"><div class="Grid-module__x1ukXq__grid Grid-module__x1ukXq__gap-default-md" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(12, 1fr)"><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-lg-6 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-2xl-4" style="position:relative"><div style="aspect-ratio:1/1;border-radius:9999px;background-color:var(--color-brand-500);padding:1rem;display:flex;justify-content:center;width:4.5rem;height:4.5rem;margin:0 auto 1rem"><span class="text-brand-100 font-dm-sans font-bold text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0">1</span></div><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-medium leading-normal text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0.5rem">Analyze Earned Media Value Over Time</h3><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Look at EMV on a timeline to identify patterns. Seasonal spikes or unexpected dips provide vital information for future planning. Essentially, time-based analysis highlights what works and when it works best.</p></div></div><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-lg-6 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-2xl-4" style="position:relative"><div style="aspect-ratio:1/1;border-radius:9999px;background-color:var(--color-brand-500);padding:1rem;display:flex;justify-content:center;width:4.5rem;height:4.5rem;margin:0 auto 1rem"><span class="text-brand-100 font-dm-sans font-bold text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0">2</span></div><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-medium leading-normal text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0.5rem">Evaluate Earned Media Value Across Channels</h3><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Compare EMV metrics from different platforms like Facebook, Instagram, and Twitter. This helps determine which channels are most cost-effective and influential in generating organic engagement.</p></div></div><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-lg-6 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-2xl-4" style="position:relative"><div style="aspect-ratio:1/1;border-radius:9999px;background-color:var(--color-brand-500);padding:1rem;display:flex;justify-content:center;width:4.5rem;height:4.5rem;margin:0 auto 1rem"><span class="text-brand-100 font-dm-sans font-bold text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0">3</span></div><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-medium leading-normal text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0.5rem">Uncover Trends and Anomalies in Earned Media Value</h3><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Always watch for outliers. An unusually high or low EMV may indicate external factors like viral moments or competitor activity. These outliers offer unique lessons and influence strategy adjustments.</p></div></div><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-lg-6 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-2xl-4" style="position:relative"><div style="aspect-ratio:1/1;border-radius:9999px;background-color:var(--color-brand-500);padding:1rem;display:flex;justify-content:center;width:4.5rem;height:4.5rem;margin:0 auto 1rem"><span class="text-brand-100 font-dm-sans font-bold text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0">4</span></div><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-medium leading-normal text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0.5rem">Put Earned Media Value in Context </h3><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Context is key. Reporting EMV in isolation is akin to showing a movie still instead of the whole film. Align EMV with related KPIs like engagement rates or ROI to give clients a full view of campaign health.</p></div></div><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-lg-6 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-2xl-4" style="position:relative"><div style="aspect-ratio:1/1;border-radius:9999px;background-color:var(--color-brand-500);padding:1rem;display:flex;justify-content:center;width:4.5rem;height:4.5rem;margin:0 auto 1rem"><span class="text-brand-100 font-dm-sans font-bold text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0">5</span></div><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-medium leading-normal text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0.5rem">Visualize Earned Media Value Performance</h3><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Humans are visual creatures, and a picture is worth a thousand words—or, in this case, dollars. Utilize charts or graphs to make EMV data visually digestible, helping clients instantly grasp performance levels.</p></div></div><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-lg-6 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-2xl-4" style="position:relative"><div style="aspect-ratio:1/1;border-radius:9999px;background-color:var(--color-brand-500);padding:1rem;display:flex;justify-content:center;width:4.5rem;height:4.5rem;margin:0 auto 1rem"><span class="text-brand-100 font-dm-sans font-bold text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0">6</span></div><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-medium leading-normal text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0.5rem">Align Earned Media Value to Client Goals</h3><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Every client is goal-oriented. Demonstrating how EMV pushes the needle toward those goals provides more proof of agency value. Align EMV metrics directly with client objectives to make the impact crystal clear.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div><section class="Section-module__O9PlXa__section Section-module__O9PlXa__padding-top-default-default Section-module__O9PlXa__padding-bottom-default-default Section-module__O9PlXa__border-radius-none" style="position:relative;overflow:visible"><div class="Container-module__V75Bgq__container"><div class="flex gap-96 flex-col w-full"><header class="flex gap-12 flex-col w-full"><div><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:65rem"><h2 class="text-black font-geologica font-medium leading-tight text-3xl md:text-5xl text-center" style="margin-bottom:0"><span>FAQs About Earned Media Value (EMV) and Earned Media Campaigns</span></h2></div></div><div class="Section-module__O9PlXa__subheading"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:65rem"><p class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-normal leading-normal text-sm md:text-md text-center" style="margin-bottom:0"><span>Still wondering how earned media drives real value? These FAQs explain how EMV compares to paid metrics, how to measure exposure across social media or press coverage, and why these authentic impressions build brand credibility more powerfully than ad campaigns.</span></p></div></div></header><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:56rem"><ul class="flex gap-24 flex-col w-full"><li fa-chevron-down " role="img" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 512 512" color="var(--color-zinc-900)"><path fill="currentColor" d="M233.4 406.6c12.5 12.5 32.8 12.5 45.3 0l192-192c12.5-12.5 12.5-32.8 0-45.3s-32.8-12.5-45.3 0L256 338.7 86.6 169.4c-12.5-12.5-32.8-12.5-45.3 0s-12.5 32.8 0 45.3l192 192z"></path></svg></div></div></button><div id="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvMWVEMnVRVGV3Y3hSNDZTejdiT2FnQw==" aria-labelledby="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvMWVEMnVRVGV3Y3hSNDZTejdiT2FnQw==" style="opacity:0;height:0px;overflow:hidden"><div class="FaqAnswer-module__BxciGG__answer"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Earned media delivers increased brand awareness, brand credibility, and performance without adding to your advertising budget. It builds trust through third-party content like reviews, press coverage, or influencer endorsements. The downside? It’s unpredictable. You can’t fully control message timing, tone, or reach, and results often rely on consistent content creation and strong communication channels. </p></div></div></div></li><li class="FaqItem-module__AvvdFG__item"><button id="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNk1JYk82N3JDRTVLRHJTcHh3ZVNQdw==" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNk1JYk82N3JDRTVLRHJTcHh3ZVNQdw==" class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__question"><div class="flex gap-24 flex-row items-center justify-between w-full"><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-semibold leading-normal text-lg">What is the difference between EMV and CPM?</h3><div class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__circle"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="chevron-down" class="svg-inline--fa fa-chevron-down " role="img" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 512 512" color="var(--color-zinc-900)"><path fill="currentColor" d="M233.4 406.6c12.5 12.5 32.8 12.5 45.3 0l192-192c12.5-12.5 12.5-32.8 0-45.3s-32.8-12.5-45.3 0L256 338.7 86.6 169.4c-12.5-12.5-32.8-12.5-45.3 0s-12.5 32.8 0 45.3l192 192z"></path></svg></div></div></button><div id="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNk1JYk82N3JDRTVLRHJTcHh3ZVNQdw==" aria-labelledby="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNk1JYk82N3JDRTVLRHJTcHh3ZVNQdw==" style="opacity:0;height:0px;overflow:hidden"><div class="FaqAnswer-module__BxciGG__answer"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>EMV reflects the value generated through attention gained without direct cost—often across third-party sites or influencer campaigns.  It measures the dollar amount equivalent of exposure from unpaid sources like news articles, social mentions, or word-of-mouth recommendations. Although often used to calculate earned media value, CPM is more commonly used as a bidding strategy which calculates advertising costs per thousand impressions. </p></div></div></div></li><li class="FaqItem-module__AvvdFG__item"><button id="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvM3NYSlFqeXlObEZzRFIwd2tGZVdhYw==" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvM3NYSlFqeXlObEZzRFIwd2tGZVdhYw==" class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__question"><div class="flex gap-24 flex-row items-center justify-between w-full"><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-semibold leading-normal text-lg">What are the five most common examples of earned media?</h3><div class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__circle"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="chevron-down" class="svg-inline--fa fa-chevron-down " role="img" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 512 512" color="var(--color-zinc-900)"><path fill="currentColor" d="M233.4 406.6c12.5 12.5 32.8 12.5 45.3 0l192-192c12.5-12.5 12.5-32.8 0-45.3s-32.8-12.5-45.3 0L256 338.7 86.6 169.4c-12.5-12.5-32.8-12.5-45.3 0s-12.5 32.8 0 45.3l192 192z"></path></svg></div></div></button><div id="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvM3NYSlFqeXlObEZzRFIwd2tGZVdhYw==" aria-labelledby="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvM3NYSlFqeXlObEZzRFIwd2tGZVdhYw==" style="opacity:0;height:0px;overflow:hidden"><div class="FaqAnswer-module__BxciGG__answer"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Typical examples include:</p><ul><li><p>News articles and press coverage from media outlets</p></li><li><p>Mentions or reposts on social channels</p></li><li><p>Online reviews and third-party posts</p></li><li><p>Influencer endorsements or creator economy features</p></li><li><p>Organic search activity</p></li></ul></div></div></div></li><li class="FaqItem-module__AvvdFG__item"><button id="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvMnV2YW9QTUEwemFSam41RGpTd2xVQg==" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvMnV2YW9QTUEwemFSam41RGpTd2xVQg==" class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__question"><div class="flex gap-24 flex-row items-center justify-between w-full"><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-semibold leading-normal text-lg">Why is earned media a valuable metric to track?</h3><div class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__circle"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="chevron-down" class="svg-inline--fa fa-chevron-down " role="img" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 512 512" color="var(--color-zinc-900)"><path fill="currentColor" d="M233.4 406.6c12.5 12.5 32.8 12.5 45.3 0l192-192c12.5-12.5 12.5-32.8 0-45.3s-32.8-12.5-45.3 0L256 338.7 86.6 169.4c-12.5-12.5-32.8-12.5-45.3 0s-12.5 32.8 0 45.3l192 192z"></path></svg></div></div></button><div id="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvMnV2YW9QTUEwemFSam41RGpTd2xVQg==" aria-labelledby="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvMnV2YW9QTUEwemFSam41RGpTd2xVQg==" style="opacity:0;height:0px;overflow:hidden"><div class="FaqAnswer-module__BxciGG__answer"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Earned media content is often used to drive brand credibility and increase brand awareness at minimal cost. It delivers authentic voice through third‑party mentions and online reviews that often is typically more authentic than paid campaigns. Tracking EMV provides meaningful performance metrics by quantifying the reach and impact of non-paid media impressions. </p></div></div></div></li><li class="FaqItem-module__AvvdFG__item"><button id="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvQTRrSElwbG8wbWRnalRGMnlha0tJ" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvQTRrSElwbG8wbWRnalRGMnlha0tJ" class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__question"><div class="flex gap-24 flex-row items-center justify-between w-full"><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-semibold leading-normal text-lg">How do earned media campaigns strengthen a brand’s credibility?</h3><div class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__circle"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="chevron-down" class="svg-inline--fa fa-chevron-down " role="img" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 512 512" color="var(--color-zinc-900)"><path fill="currentColor" d="M233.4 406.6c12.5 12.5 32.8 12.5 45.3 0l192-192c12.5-12.5 12.5-32.8 0-45.3s-32.8-12.5-45.3 0L256 338.7 86.6 169.4c-12.5-12.5-32.8-12.5-45.3 0s-12.5 32.8 0 45.3l192 192z"></path></svg></div></div></button><div id="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvQTRrSElwbG8wbWRnalRGMnlha0tJ" aria-labelledby="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvQTRrSElwbG8wbWRnalRGMnlha0tJ" style="opacity:0;height:0px;overflow:hidden"><div class="FaqAnswer-module__BxciGG__answer"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Campaigns that result in third-party content or influencer endorsements give weight to your client’s brand, boosting perception of authenticity and trust. Positive press coverage, online reviews, or social mentions create meaningful connections with potential customers—beyond what advertising alone can achieve. </p></div></div></div></li><li class="FaqItem-module__AvvdFG__item"><button id="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNm92UUYwQVBvQ3FIamN4Y1pyOG01YQ==" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNm92UUYwQVBvQ3FIamN4Y1pyOG01YQ==" class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__question"><div class="flex gap-24 flex-row items-center justify-between w-full"><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-semibold leading-normal text-lg">Can earned media value calculations align with social media strategy and influencer campaigns?</h3><div class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__circle"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="chevron-down" class="svg-inline--fa fa-chevron-down " role="img" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 512 512" color="var(--color-zinc-900)"><path fill="currentColor" d="M233.4 406.6c12.5 12.5 32.8 12.5 45.3 0l192-192c12.5-12.5 12.5-32.8 0-45.3s-32.8-12.5-45.3 0L256 338.7 86.6 169.4c-12.5-12.5-32.8-12.5-45.3 0s-12.5 32.8 0 45.3l192 192z"></path></svg></div></div></button><div id="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNm92UUYwQVBvQ3FIamN4Y1pyOG01YQ==" aria-labelledby="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNm92UUYwQVBvQ3FIamN4Y1pyOG01YQ==" style="opacity:0;height:0px;overflow:hidden"><div class="FaqAnswer-module__BxciGG__answer"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Absolutely. Earned media campaign ROI can be calculated by applying EMV formulas—like EMV impressions × CPM rate—to social media content or influencer endorsements. This approach quantifies the value of organic engagement in a way that complements paid social ads and integrates smoothly with your broader social media marketing strategy. </p></div></div></div></li><li class="FaqItem-module__AvvdFG__item"><button id="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNVA4YWQ3Q1pld1BhVFNUTlVWVnM0Yw==" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNVA4YWQ3Q1pld1BhVFNUTlVWVnM0Yw==" class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__question"><div class="flex gap-24 flex-row items-center justify-between w-full"><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-semibold leading-normal text-lg">How do adjustment factors impact earned media value?</h3><div class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__circle"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="chevron-down" class="svg-inline--fa fa-chevron-down " role="img" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 512 512" color="var(--color-zinc-900)"><path fill="currentColor" d="M233.4 406.6c12.5 12.5 32.8 12.5 45.3 0l192-192c12.5-12.5 12.5-32.8 0-45.3s-32.8-12.5-45.3 0L256 338.7 86.6 169.4c-12.5-12.5-32.8-12.5-45.3 0s-12.5 32.8 0 45.3l192 192z"></path></svg></div></div></button><div id="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNVA4YWQ3Q1pld1BhVFNUTlVWVnM0Yw==" aria-labelledby="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvNVA4YWQ3Q1pld1BhVFNUTlVWVnM0Yw==" style="opacity:0;height:0px;overflow:hidden"><div class="FaqAnswer-module__BxciGG__answer"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Adjustment factors help fine-tune EMV to reflect campaign objectives, content quality, and context—whether that content appears on news outlets, third-party sites, or social media channels. These modifiers ensure that the calculated dollar amount reflects differences in media impressions, the brand’s image, or the quality of earned media content. </p></div></div></div></li><li class="FaqItem-module__AvvdFG__item"><button id="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvMnNjQ2RvSlJ5VWhlN0xWdzBCUkhCMQ==" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvMnNjQ2RvSlJ5VWhlN0xWdzBCUkhCMQ==" class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__question"><div class="flex gap-24 flex-row items-center justify-between w-full"><h3 class="text-zinc-900 font-dm-sans font-semibold leading-normal text-lg">Why do some earned media campaigns deliver better engagement than paid efforts?</h3><div class="FaqQuestion-module__hwIuUG__circle"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="chevron-down" class="svg-inline--fa fa-chevron-down " role="img" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 512 512" color="var(--color-zinc-900)"><path fill="currentColor" d="M233.4 406.6c12.5 12.5 32.8 12.5 45.3 0l192-192c12.5-12.5 12.5-32.8 0-45.3s-32.8-12.5-45.3 0L256 338.7 86.6 169.4c-12.5-12.5-32.8-12.5-45.3 0s-12.5 32.8 0 45.3l192 192z"></path></svg></div></div></button><div id="faq-answer-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvMnNjQ2RvSlJ5VWhlN0xWdzBCUkhCMQ==" aria-labelledby="faq-question-MTpjcm46Y29udGVudGZ1bDo6OmNvbnRlbnQ6c3BhY2VzL2RmY3ZrejZqODU5ai9lbnZpcm9ubWVudHMvbWFzdGVyL2VudHJpZXMvMnNjQ2RvSlJ5VWhlN0xWdzBCUkhCMQ==" style="opacity:0;height:0px;overflow:hidden"><div class="FaqAnswer-module__BxciGG__answer"><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Because earned media’s power lies in audience trust—content earned through relationships or positive sentiment often receives more attention than paid placements. Earned media creates authentic exposure across third-party mentions and social channels, enhancing customer connections with a real voice and delivering stronger engagement metrics than many paid tactics. </p></div></div></div></li></ul></div></div></div></section></div></section><section data-section-bg="zinc-50" class="Section-module__O9PlXa__section Section-module__O9PlXa__padding-top-default-default Section-module__O9PlXa__padding-bottom-default-default bg-zinc-50 Section-module__O9PlXa__border-radius-none" style="position:relative;overflow:visible"><div class="flex gap-40 flex-col w-full"><div class="Container-module__V75Bgq__container"><header class="flex gap-12 flex-col w-full"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:30rem"><div class=""><div class="text-brand-500 font-dm-sans font-semibold leading-tight text-sm md:text-md text-center uppercase">Reporting on Earned Media Value</div></div></div><div><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:65rem"><h2 class="text-black font-geologica font-medium leading-tight text-3xl md:text-4xl text-center" style="margin-bottom:0">Facebook Dashboard Example</h2></div></div><div class="Section-module__O9PlXa__subheading"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:48rem"><div class="text-black font-dm-sans font-normal leading-normal text-lg text-center" style="margin-bottom:0">AgencyAnalytics&#x27; pre-built Facebook report templates highlight key metrics such as engagement, reach, and ROI, providing an integrated view of a campaign&#x27;s efficacy. The platform&#x27;s versatility allows for a mix-and-match approach to data presentation and configures the dashboard to spotlight Earned Media Value alongside other KPIs. This creates a holistic picture beyond mere numbers and turns into actionable insights.</div></div></div></header><div class="Section-module__O9PlXa__cta Section-module__O9PlXa__cta-margin-md"><div These three tips are the playbook for improvement, each offering a unique avenue for optimization.</p></header></div><div class="Section-module__O9PlXa__body" style="text-align:center"><div class="MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__maxWidth MaxWidth-module__LUOD7q__center" style="--maxWidth:65rem"><div class="Grid-module__x1ukXq__grid Grid-module__x1ukXq__gap-default-md" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(12, 1fr)"><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-lg-6 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-2xl-4" style="position:relative"><div style="aspect-ratio:1/1;border-radius:9999px;background-color:var(--color-brand-800);padding:1rem;display:flex;justify-content:center;width:4.5rem;height:4.5rem;margin:0 auto 1rem"><span class="text-white font-dm-sans font-bold text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0">1</span></div><h3 class="text-white font-dm-sans font-medium leading-normal text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0.5rem">Improve Content Quality</h3><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Quality content engages the audience and encourages sharing. A higher share rate directly correlates with a spike in Earned Media Value.</p></div></div><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-lg-6 GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-2xl-4" style="position:relative"><div style="aspect-ratio:1/1;border-radius:9999px;background-color:var(--color-brand-800);padding:1rem;display:flex;justify-content:center;width:4.5rem;height:4.5rem;margin:0 auto 1rem"><span class="text-white font-dm-sans font-bold text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0">2</span></div><h3 class="text-white font-dm-sans font-medium leading-normal text-3xl" style="margin-bottom:0.5rem">  Time Posts Wisely</h3><div class="Prose-module__XZJZFq__prose"><p>Post content when the target audience is most active. Analytics tools help identify these key time slots, aligning them with higher engagement and, in turn, a higher Earned Media Value.</p></div></div><div class="GridItem-module__k_88Jq__span-default-12 advertising budget. It builds trust through third-party content like reviews, press coverage, or influencer endorsements. The downside? It’s unpredictable. You can’t fully control message timing, tone, or reach, and results often rely on consistent content creation and strong communication channels. \"}},{\"@type\":\"Question\",\"name\":\"What is the difference between EMV and CPM?\",\"acceptedAnswer\":{\"@type\":\"Answer\",\"text\":\"EMV reflects the value generated through attention gained without direct cost—often across third-party sites or influencer campaigns.  It measures the dollar amount equivalent of exposure from unpaid sources like news articles, social mentions, or word-of-mouth recommendations. Although often used to calculate earned media value, CPM is more commonly used as a bidding strategy which calculates advertising costs per thousand impressions. \"}},{\"@type\":\"Question\",\"name\":\"What are the five most common examples of earned media?\",\"acceptedAnswer\":{\"@type\":\"Answer\",\"text\":\"Typical examples include: News articles and press coverage from media outlets Mentions or reposts on social channels Online reviews and third-party posts Influencer endorsements or creator economy features Organic search activity \"}},{\"@type\":\"Question\",\"name\":\"Why is earned media a valuable metric to track?\",\"acceptedAnswer\":{\"@type\":\"Answer\",\"text\":\"Earned media content is often used to drive brand credibility and increase brand awareness at minimal cost. It delivers authentic voice through third‑party mentions and online reviews that often is typically more authentic than paid campaigns. Tracking EMV provides meaningful performance metrics by quantifying the reach and impact of non-paid media impressions. \"}},{\"@type\":\"Question\",\"name\":\"How do earned media campaigns strengthen a brand’s credibility?\",\"acceptedAnswer\":{\"@type\":\"Answer\",\"text\":\"Campaigns that result in third-party content or influencer endorsements give weight to your client’s brand, boosting perception of authenticity and trust. Positive press coverage, online reviews, or social mentions create meaningful connections with potential customers—beyond what advertising alone can achieve. \"}},{\"@type\":\"Question\",\"name\":\"Can earned media value calculations align with social media strategy and influencer campaigns?\",\"acceptedAnswer\":{\"@type\":\"Answer\",\"text\":\"Absolutely. Earned media campaign ROI can be calculated by applying EMV formulas—like EMV impressions × CPM rate—to social media content or influencer endorsements. This approach quantifies the value of organic engagement in a way that complements paid social ads and integrates smoothly with your broader social media marketing strategy. \"}},{\"@type\":\"Question\",\"name\":\"How do adjustment factors impact earned media value?\",\"acceptedAnswer\":{\"@type\":\"Answer\",\"text\":\"Adjustment factors help fine-tune EMV to reflect campaign objectives, content quality, and context—whether that content appears on news outlets, third-party sites, or social media channels. These modifiers ensure that the calculated dollar amount reflects differences in media impressions, the brand’s image, or the quality of earned media content. \"}},{\"@type\":\"Question\",\"name\":\"Why do some earned media campaigns deliver better engagement than paid efforts?\",\"acceptedAnswer\":{\"@type\":\"Answer\",\"text\":\"Because earned media’s power lies in audience trust—content earned through relationships or positive sentiment often receives more attention than paid placements. Earned media creates authentic exposure across third-party mentions and social channels, enhancing customer connections with a real voice and delivering stronger engagement metrics than many paid tactics. \"}}]},{\"@type\":\"Review\",\"author\":{\"@type\":\"Person\",\"name\":\"Mark Mehler\",\"worksFor\":{\"@type\":\"Organization\",\"name\":\"Back2Black Marketing Agency\"}},\"datePublished\":\"2023-01-18T16:50:19.377Z\",\"reviewBody\":\"This is exactly the type of tool I have been looking for.
